# Known differences between the FIX 5.0 SP2 dictionary derived from the FIX
# Orchestra repository OrchestraFIXLatest.xml (FIX Latest EP312, filtered by
# pedigree with Dictionary::from_orchestra_latest_xml) and the embedded
# QuickFIX dictionary spec/FIX50SP2.xml, one per line, exactly as
# tests/orchestra_equivalence.rs prints them. `orchestra-only` is in the
# derived dictionary only, `quickfix-only` in the QuickFIX file only,
# `conflict` in both with different content; the policy is that Orchestra
# wins. doc/orchestra_differences.md summarises and justifies each class.
# A line here that the diff no longer finds fails the test, as does a
# difference missing from here. Lines starting with `#` are comments.

# Field names: Orchestra corrects QuickFIX's misspellings (Referemce,
# Arrangment) and uses the current names of 327 and 1225.

conflict field 2884 name: orchestra ContraRoutingArrangementIndicator quickfix ContraRoutingArrangmentIndicator
conflict field 327 name: orchestra HaltReason quickfix HaltReasonInt
